# External-sector scorecard, every country 1995-2024 (World Bank)

Escrow esc_379d14f823434fa18ba47212, job job_8d4d75dfb9aa4068. All scorecard figures, rankings and lists below are computed from **macro-external-pull3.csv** (the latest pull). Periods 1995-2004, 2005-2014, 2015-2024; mean over years with a value, n = years used, n/a = no value in the period; change = last-period mean minus first-period mean (n/a if either is n/a). Units: percent (growth) / percent of GDP (shares). Figures rounded to 2 decimals, half away from zero, from unrounded values. Aggregates (region id NA) excluded.

Pull status, per the TASK. TASK lines: "A request failing after one retry is not available with what was tried (counts as done)." and "A pull whose every request fails after one retry: attach a header-only CSV ... pull k failed". PULL 2 is NOT a failed pull: its country-list request succeeded (HTTP 200), so not every request failed, and its full-row CSV (6510 rows) is attached. Each of its 3 indicator requests failed after one retry (UTC times and errors in PROOF > PULL 2 requests), so each is not available with what was tried (counts as done) and its cells are empty in macro-external-pull2.csv. PULL 1 and PULL 3: every request succeeded. So the Changes sections compare PULL 2 vs PULL 1 and PULL 3 vs PULL 2, matched by key, each listing every changed cell.

Data files: macro-external-pull1.csv rows=6510 sha256=3e3dd84d4713f4d155c33c62a69447d320a48bebd30230e0eff3297ff81ce1c2; macro-external-pull2.csv rows=6510 sha256=b07e30751f8563a9840b4a2c414786bcd03ffce01056bfa16e302f8211f5f737; macro-external-pull3.csv rows=6510 sha256=3e3dd84d4713f4d155c33c62a69447d320a48bebd30230e0eff3297ff81ce1c2. 217 countries x 30 years = 6510 rows. ## Changes: PULL 3 vs PULL 2

Matched by key countryiso3code|date between macro-external-pull2.csv (earlier) and macro-external-pull3.csv (later); compared columns (all after date): export growth, investment share, current account.

Row counts: PULL 2 = 6510, PULL 3 = 6510; new (key only in PULL 3) = 0; gone (key only in PULL 2) = 0; both (key in both pulls) = 6510; of those, changed = 5859 keys, unchanged = 651 keys. Identity: 6510 = 6510 + 0 - 0.

Changed cells: 14854 in total (export growth 4652, investment share 5046, current account 5156).

new: none

gone: none

changed: every changed cell, one line per changed key, in this format: `key|export growth|investment share|current account`, where each column shows `earlier>later` (exact unrounded CSV values; `_` = empty cell) when that cell changed, and `=` when it did not.
